Specifically, PercepCap follows a perceive-describe generation chain, where the model first produces a spatiotemporal perception trace comprising object trajectories and temporal events, and then generates the final caption conditioned on the perceived evidence. To support this, we design a two-stage training strategy. Perceive-then-Describe Supervised Fine-tuning adapts the model from caption-only generation to the proposed perceive-describe chain, while Perception-Grounded Reinforcement Learning optimizes perception trace and caption quality with joint rewards over perception chain and the final caption. To support our two-stage training, we introduce Caption-Anchored Perception Data Construction. This pipeline builds the SFT and RL training data by first generating a caption-only description, extracting the objects and events it mentions, and grounding them back in the video with boxes and timestamps. This yields caption-aligned perception data that provides solid training ground truth, ensuring that the explicit perception trace and final caption refer to the same objects and events. Across direct caption and caption-to-QA evaluation, PercepCap consistently improves upon the Qwen3-VL baseline and demonstrates leading caption quality.

</details>


### [71] [ATSplat: Compact Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting with Adaptive Token Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20417)
*Cho In,Jeonghwan Cho,Mijin Yoo,Gim Hee Lee,Seon Joo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ATSplat提出了一种前馈式3D高斯泼溅框架，通过自适应3D令牌恢复场景自适应容量分配能力，相比密集前馈方法减少了5.7倍以上的高斯数量，同时达到领先渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈3DGS方法将高斯原语固定在输入像素上沿光线提升，导致原语数量和位置依赖图像分辨率和视角而非场景复杂度，产生大量冗余高斯，丧失了3DGS优化中的自适应容量分配能力。

Method: 1) 将粗粒度块级深度和相机线索提升为稀疏3D锚点令牌，形成紧凑场景骨架；2) 每个令牌回归为局部高斯（含可学习3D偏移），解耦原语放置与输入图像网格；3) 自适应令牌扩展模块通过渲染误差图监督预测令牌级不确定性，选择性扩展高不确定性令牌。

Result: 在RealEstate10K和DL3DV数据集上，ATSplat达到SOTA渲染质量，高斯数量减少5.7倍以上；12张512×960输入图像可在单GPU上1秒内完成重建，以311K高斯实现1136 FPS的高质量新视角渲染。

Conclusion: ATSplat通过稀疏到自适应的范式有效恢复了3DGS的自适应分配能力，在显著压缩高斯数量的同时保持甚至提升了渲染质量，实现了高效的前馈式新视角合成。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) achieves high-quality novel-view synthesis by optimizing freely placed primitives in 3D and adaptively densifying them in under-reconstructed regions. However, this scene-adaptive capacity allocation is largely lost in existing feed-forward 3DGS methods, which commonly regress Gaussians at input pixels and lift them along camera rays. Such pixel-aligned formulations make the number and placement of primitives depend on image resolution and input viewpoints rather than scene complexity, resulting in dense and often redundant Gaussian sets. We present ATSplat, a feed-forward 3DGS framework that restores the adaptive allocation capability of 3DGS optimization through Adaptive 3D Tokens. ATSplat first lifts coarse patch-level depth and camera cues into sparse 3D anchor tokens, forming a compact scaffold of the scene. Each token is then regressed into local Gaussians with learnable 3D offsets, decoupling primitive placement from input image grids. An Adaptive Token Expansion module predicts a token-level uncertainty score, supervised by rendering error maps, and selectively expands high-uncertainty tokens through learnable expansion layers. This sparse-to-adaptive formulation enables ATSplat to concentrate primitives in challenging regions while maintaining a compact representation. Experiments on two representative datasets, RealEstate10K and DL3DV, show that ATSplat achieves state-of-the-art rendering quality while reducing the number of Gaussians by more than $5.7\times$ compared with dense feed-forward 3DGS methods. From 12 input images at $512 \times 960$ resolution, ATSplat completes reconstruction in less than a second using a single commercial GPU, and renders high-quality novel views at 1136 FPS ($512 \times 960$) with only 311K Gaussians.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [72] [FineServe: A Fine-Grained Dataset and Characterization of Global LLM Serving Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19349)
*Tiancheng Zhang,Shaoyuan Huang,Mingyuan Wang,Yunfeng Zhao,Xiaofei Wang,Wenyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FineServe是一个从全球商业平台收集的多模型LLM服务真实工作负载数据集，揭示了不同模型架构和任务下到达动态和令牌行为的波动规律，并提供了可配置的工作负载生成器用于评估多模型服务系统的路由、调度和容量规划策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务研究依赖代理轨迹或粗粒度表征，无法捕捉现代多模型LLM平台的异构性，缺乏对真实世界服务动态的深入理解。

Method: 从全球商业平台收集真实多模型LLM服务工作负载数据（FineServe数据集），对到达动态和令牌行为进行细粒度分析，并开发可配置的工作负载生成器。

Result: 发现不同模型架构、规模和任务意图下存在根本不同的波动规律，并构建了支持细粒度模型感知工作负载组合的生成器。

Conclusion: FineServe为评估多模型LLM服务系统中的路由、调度和容量规划策略提供了真实的基础，填补了现有工作负载基准的空白。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as always-on online services, making efficient LLM serving a critical systems challenge. Achieving low latency and high throughput under volatile demand requires deep understanding of real-world serving workloads, yet existing studies often rely on proxy traces or coarse-grained characterizations that fail to capture the heterogeneity of modern multi-model LLM platforms. We present FineServe, an in-the-wild, multi-model LLM serving workload dataset collected from a global commercial marketplace, enabling fine-grained characterization of real-world serving dynamics across heterogeneous models and tasks. Leveraging FineServe, we conduct a comprehensive analysis of arrival dynamics and token behavior, revealing fundamentally different fluctuation regimes across model architectures, scales and task intents. Building on these insights, we develop the FineServe workload generator, which composes fine-grained model-aware workloads into configurable mixtures tailored for benchmarking multi-model serving platforms. By exposing these fine-grained workload dynamics, FineServe provides a realistic foundation for evaluating routing, scheduling, and capacity-planning strategies in LLM serving systems. FineServe is available at https://github.com/hihiztc1/FineServe.

</details>


### [73] [Hybrid LSTM-Graph Neural Framework for Robust Financial Fraud Detection and Adversarial Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19350)
*Mariam Zakaria Moussa Ali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 FraudShield AI，一种融合 LSTM 与图拓扑特征的混合框架，用于检测金融洗钱模式（如 smurfing 和 layering），在 PaySim 数据集上显著优于逻辑回归和 XGBoost 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融机构在检测复杂洗钱模式时面临极端数据不平衡（欺诈率仅 0.13%）和对抗性规避策略的挑战，传统方法难以有效捕捉时间序列和结构性上下文。

Method: 提出 FraudShield AI 混合框架：1) LSTM 模块捕捉交易时间序列动态；2) 手工设计的图拓扑特征（PageRank 中心性、入度动态、自定义流动比率）进行网络级取证分析；3) 使用 Focal Loss 处理类别不平衡；4) 引入动态阈值机制增强对低价值 smurfing 攻击的鲁棒性。

Result: 在 PaySim 数据集上，混合模型在 Precision、Recall 和 F1-Score 上全面超越逻辑回归和 XGBoost 基线，尤其对难以检测的微交易欺诈模式表现优异。消融实验验证了时序和拓扑组件具有互补贡献。

Conclusion: 将时序建模与图拓扑特征融合是一种有效的金融欺诈检测范式，网络级取证分析比孤立交易分析更具优势，Focal Loss 和动态阈值机制进一步增强了模型对极端不平衡和低价值攻击的适应性。

Abstract: Financial institutions face significant challenges in detecting sophisticated money laundering patterns, such as smurfing and layering, due to extreme data imbalance (0.13% fraud rate) and evolving adversarial evasion tactics. This paper proposes FraudShield AI, a hybrid framework that integrates Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks with hand-crafted Graph Topological Features to capture both temporal sequences and structural relational context. By engineering network-centric features including PageRank Centrality, In-Degree dynamics, and a custom Flow Ratio, the system shifts the detection paradigm from isolated transaction analysis to network-level forensics. A Focal Loss objective is used to address class imbalance, and a dynamic thresholding mechanism is introduced to improve resilience against low-value smurfing attacks. Experimental evaluation on the PaySim dataset shows that the proposed hybrid model substantially outperforms Logistic Regression and XGBoost baselines in Precision, Recall, and F1-Score, particularly on hard-to-detect micro-transaction fraud patterns. An ablation study confirms the complementary contribution of both the temporal and topological components.

</details>


### [74] [OpenEvoShield: Dual Non-Stationary Continual Defense for Open-World Multi-Agent System Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19351)
*Litian Zhang,Chaozhuo Li,Yuting Zhang,Zejian Chen,Bingyu Yan,Qiwei Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenEvoShield，一种面向LLM多智能体系统的共演化持续防御框架，通过非对称速率控制器、动态行为边界、EWC正则化策略集成和能量基多粒度检测器，有效应对对抗性攻击与正常行为漂移的双重动态威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法将部署视为封闭世界问题，无法应对攻击策略与正常行为双重动态漂移，一旦分布超出训练覆盖范围性能急剧下降。

Method: 提出四模块框架：(M1)非对称速率控制器解耦攻击侧与正常侧学习速率；(M2)正常边界更新器维持慢速动态行为边界；(M3)EWC正则化策略集成实现快速适应且不灾难性遗忘；(M4)能量基多粒度检测器融合节点、子图、图级证据检测OOD攻击。

Result: 在5个基准和4种MAS拓扑上经过100轮部署实验，优于静态和持续基线，能够检测大多数未见攻击同时保持低误报率。

Conclusion: OpenEvoShield通过共演化持续防御机制有效应对LLM-MAS中攻击与正常行为双重动态漂移，为安全关键场景提供了可行的防御方案。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (LLM-MAS) are increasingly deployed in safety-critical applications, where adversaries inject malicious instructions through inter-agent communication to propagate harmful behaviors. Unlike static threats, these attacks are doubly dynamic: adversaries refine injection strategies against deployed defenses while normal-agent behavior drifts with system expansion. Existing defenses treat deployment as a closed-world problem and degrade rapidly once either distribution shifts beyond training coverage. We propose OpenEvoShield, a co-evolutionary continual defense framework for LLM-MAS. An asymmetric rate controller (M1) decouples fast attack-side and slow normal-side learning rates from dual drift signals. A normal-boundary updater (M2) maintains a dynamic behavioral boundary at the slow rate, while an EWC-regularized policy ensemble (M3) fast-adapts without catastrophic forgetting. An energy-based multi-granularity detector (M4) fuses node-, subgraph-, and graph-level evidence to classify novel attacks as out-of-distribution. Experiments over 100 deployment rounds across five benchmarks and four MAS topologies show that OpenEvoShield outperforms static and continual baselines, detecting most previously unseen attacks while keeping false positive rates low.

</details>


### [75] [Benchmarking Confidential GPU Inference on NVIDIA H100 under Intel TDX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19353)
*Wei Wang,Abdul Hyee Waqas,Burns Smith*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对NVIDIA H100 GPU在Intel TDX机密计算环境下的LLM推理性能进行了基准测试，发现机密模式会导致TTFT增加21.8%-27.8%，全局token吞吐量下降17.7%-21.1%，且大模型更早达到性能饱和拐点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机密计算正成为AI推理的部署需求，但启用GPU机密执行对LLM服务性能的影响仍不明确，缺乏系统性的量化基准研究。

Method: 在单块NVIDIA H100 80GB GPU（Intel TDX机密实例）上，对比标准非机密执行与机密计算模式，使用Mistral-7B和Qwen3-30B-A3B两个模型，测量TTFT、端到端延迟、token吞吐量等多项指标，并设置固定请求率和闭环并发两种实验场景。

Result: 机密模式下Mistral-7B平均TTFT增加21.8%，Qwen3-30B-A3B增加27.8%；全局token吞吐量分别下降17.7%和21.1%。闭环并发实验中吞吐量差距保持在11.5-20.2%范围，但大模型在机密模式下更早达到性能饱和拐点。

Conclusion: 机密GPU推理在负载下仍可保持可用吞吐量，但容量规划必须同时考虑稳态吞吐量惩罚和大模型更早出现的性能饱和行为。

Abstract: Confidential computing is becoming a practical deployment requirement for AI inference workloads that process sensitive inputs or protect proprietary model assets. However, the performance cost of enabling confidential execution for GPU-accelerated large language model serving remains workload dependent and operationally important. This paper presents a benchmark study comparing standard non-confidential execution with confidential computing mode on a single NVIDIA H100 80GB GPU hosted in an Intel TDX confidential instance. The evaluation uses two representative language models, Mistral-7B v0.1 and Qwen3-30B-A3B, and measures time to first token, end-to-end request latency, per-request token generation throughput, global token throughput, and closed-loop request throughput under increasing concurrency. In fixed request-rate experiments, confidential mode increases average TTFT by 21.8% for Mistral-7B and 27.8% for Qwen3-30B-A3B, while global token throughput drops by 17.7% and 21.1%, respectively. In closed-loop concurrency experiments, throughput gaps remain in the 11.5-20.2% range, but the larger model reaches its saturation knee earlier under confidential mode. The results suggest that confidential GPU inference can retain usable throughput under load, but capacity planning must account for both the steady throughput penalty and the earlier saturation behavior observed for larger models.

</details>


### [76] [FormulaSPIN: Self-Play Fine-Tuning for Natural Language to Spreadsheet Formula Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19354)
*Cy Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了FORMULASPIN框架，通过自对弈方式突破电子表格公式生成的性能天花板，无需额外标注数据，在NL2FORMULA任务上达到74.9%精确匹配和87.1%执行准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公式生成方法依赖静态监督数据，很快在有限标注上饱和；而vanilla SPIN会惩罚执行等价但形式不同的公式，产生矛盾梯度，无法直接应用。

Method: 将训练建模为双人博弈：主玩家学习偏好真实公式而非旧版本生成结果；利用公式可执行性作为隐式监督信号，按语义正确性到风格优化的自适应课程学习；引入ExecVote语义级投票机制处理多有效公式。

Result: 在NL2FORMULA上达到74.9%精确匹配和87.1%执行准确率，超越传统SFT和前沿闭源模型，达到需额外偏好标注模型的性能水平。

Conclusion: 自对弈范式在标注稀缺场景下有巨大潜力，且该方法可推广到其他可执行领域之外的任务。

Abstract: Spreadsheet applications are used by hundreds of millions worldwide, yet writing formulas remains a significant barrier. Existing approaches rely on static supervised data, which quickly saturates on limited annotations. In this paper, we introduce FORMULASPIN, a self-play framework that breaks the ceiling of supervised fine-tuning by enabling iterative self-improvement without any additional data. Vanilla SPIN fails on this task: it uniformly penalizes every non-matching output, so execution-equivalent alternatives are punished as negatives in one example while serving as ground truth in another, producing contradictory gradients. Our framework resolves this by exploiting formula generation's unique advantage: binary executability provides implicit supervision that separates semantic errors from valid stylistic variants. We frame training as a two-player game in which the main player learns to prefer ground-truth formulas over those from its previous version, while execution feedback sorts outputs into distinct granularities-enabling an adaptive curriculum that shifts from semantic correctness to stylistic refinement. To further increase accuracy, we incorporate ExecVote, a semantic-level voting mechanism that naturally handles multiple valid formulations. Experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that FORMULASPIN achieves state-of-the-art performance, with 74.9% exact match and 87.1% execution accuracy on NL2FORMULA, matching models trained with additional preference annotations while outperforming both traditional SFT and frontier proprietary models. These findings underscore self-play's potential to tackle scarce data tasks and open the door to extending it beyond executable domains.

</details>


### [77] [Information Discernment in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19355)
*Joshua Ashkinaze,Laura Kurek,Alina Faisal,Tongyuan Miao,Mariam Joseph,Ceren Budak,Eric Gilbert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出信息辨别（information discernment）概念，包含来源辨别（source discernment）和真相辨别（truth discernment）两个维度。通过Learn2Discern（L2D）框架和基准测试，对13个模型近67万次试验进行评估，发现模型在两方面均表现近随机水平。用户研究（n=299）验证了三项公理的有效性，且辨别失败会降低用户信任和使用意愿。研究发现模型更依赖来源流行度而非可靠性，更新幅度未区分改善或恶化真相的程度。新模型虽提升了真相辨别，但来源辨别仍是盲点。研究提出简单推理时干预方法可改善两种辨别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益依赖互联网等外部知识源，但它们是否能够恰当权衡信息质量——对可靠来源给予更高权重（来源辨别），以及对使先验更接近真相的主张给予更高更新（真相辨别）？现有研究缺乏对此的系统形式化评估，因此需要建立规范性框架和基准来量化模型的信息辨别能力。

Method: 1. 形式化信息辨别为三个规范性公理，设计可解释指标；2. 进行预注册、配额匹配的用户研究（n=299）验证公理的用户认可度；3. 对13个模型进行近67万次试验，评估来源辨别和真相辨别能力；4. 分析模型大小、版本差异对辨别能力的影响；5. 探索推理时干预方法改善辨别能力。

Result: 模型在来源辨别和真相辨别上均接近随机水平；模型对来源流行度的依赖是对可靠性的两倍；无论主张是改善还是恶化真相，模型更新幅度几乎相同；模型在先验最准确的数据集上整合外部知识效果最佳；新模型和大型模型提升了真相辨别但未提升来源辨别。

Conclusion: LLM在信息辨别上存在系统性缺陷，来源辨别是当前模型复杂度的盲点。用户认可规范性公理且辨别失败会降低使用意愿。简单推理时干预可改善两种辨别能力。该研究提供了一套测试基准和评估方法，对LLM替代传统搜索场景下的对齐性具有重要价值。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used with external knowledge sources like the internet. Do they weigh information appropriately -- updating more for reliable sources (source discernment) and more when claims bring priors closer to the truth (truth discernment)? We formalize this as information discernment and introduce Learn2Discern (L2D), an experimental framework and benchmark grounded in three normative axioms with interpretable metrics. To establish external validity, a pre-registered, quota-matched user study (n=299) confirms that real LLM users endorse all three axioms and report that violations reduce their trust and usage intent. Across 13 models and nearly 670K trials, we find consistent failures across both dimensions: models perform near chance on source and truth discernment, rely on source popularity twice as much as source reliability, and update roughly equally whether a claim improves or worsens their position relative to the ground truth. Models integrate external knowledge most effectively on datasets where their priors are already the most accurate. Newer and larger models improve truth discernment but not source discernment, a blind spot that model complexity does not address. We identify simple inference-time interventions that improve both forms of discernment. We release our dataset and survey as a testbed for a core alignment property that scales in importance as LLMs replace traditional search.

</details>


### [78] [NEXUS: Structured Runtime Safety for Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19356)
*Elias Hossain,Md Mehedi Hasan Nipu,Tasfia Nuzhat Ornee,Rajib Rana,Niloofar Yousefi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NEXUS是一个用于LLM Agent的运行时安全监控器，通过结合确定性规则、参数级检查和校准的逻辑回归风险评分，实现四级干预策略（允许、阻止、请求确认、请求修订），在多项基准测试中表现优异且延迟极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent在实际应用中执行高影响力动作，需要运行时安全监控来防止有害行为，但现有方法缺乏细粒度的分级干预能力。

Method: 提出NEXUS框架，结合确定性安全规则、参数级检查和校准的逻辑回归风险评分，实现四级形式化干预策略：允许、阻止、请求确认、请求修订。

Result: 在128实例合成基准上F1=0.949，4类干预准确率0.6406（比纯规则高出27.3个百分点）；R-Judge上F1=0.861；AgentHarm上与纯规则持平；IPI上0%攻击成功率；NEXUS-Stress上F1=0.881；中位延迟仅0.205ms。

Conclusion: NEXUS通过分级干预策略有效提升了LLM Agent运行时的安全性，在多项基准测试中优于纯规则方法，且计算开销极低，适合实际部署。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents increasingly execute high-impact actions, making runtime safety monitoring essential. We present NEXUS (Neural EXecution Utility and Safety), a structured-plan safety monitor that applies a formal intervention policy to select among four actions: allow, block, request confirmation, or request revision. NEXUS combines deterministic safety rules, argument-level inspection, and a calibrated logistic-regression risk score for graded escalation. On a 128-instance synthetic benchmark, NEXUS achieves an F1 score of 0.949 and a 4-class intervention accuracy of 0.6406, outperforming rule-only intervention selection by 27.3 percentage points. It also improves over rule-only on R-Judge (F1 = 0.861 vs. 0.849), matches rule-only on AgentHarm due to threat-model limits, and achieves 0% ASR at 99% control allow on IPI. On the rule-blind NEXUS-Stress benchmark, NEXUS reaches an F1 score of 0.881, highlighting the difficulty of fine-grained intervention routing. With 0.205 ms median latency, NEXUS adds under 0.1% overhead to typical agent loops. Code, benchmarks, and the calibrated risk scorer are publicly released.

</details>


### [79] [Stochastic Primal-Dual Decoding for Multiobjective Generative Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19357)
*Dmitrii Moor,Ben Carterette,Senthilkumar Krishnamoorthy,Kyle Kretschman,Denis Beslic,Melissa Yalla,Alice Y Wang,Mounia Lalmas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级的推理时解码层，通过在线约束优化（原始-对偶近似）增强自回归生成式推荐系统，使其无需重新训练即可支持多目标序列生成，并在实际系统中实现了辅助目标+1.8%的提升且不影响用户满意度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐系统在处理多目标（如公平性、属性约束）时，要么依赖后处理技术（未考虑生成式模型的序列特性），要么将辅助目标融入训练（大规模系统中不实用），缺乏轻量且适配生成式范式的在线多目标优化方法。

Method: 提出一种推理时解码层，将序列生成建模为在线约束优化问题：基于剩余约束松弛量（slack）动态调整相关性与辅助目标的权衡，采用随机原始-对偶近似方案逐项选择物品，无需修改或重新训练基础模型。

Result: 理论层面提供了约束违反和遗憾界的保证；离线实验与大规模在线A/B实验表明：在真实推荐系统中，辅助目标提升+1.8%，且用户满意度零损失，多目标权衡持续改善。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了在不重新训练模型的前提下，通过推理时优化即可有效实现生成式推荐系统的多目标序列生成，兼顾了实用性与理论保障。

Abstract: Recent advances in recommender systems (RS) have shown substantial performance gains through generative modelling. In practice, recommendation often involves constructing slates -- ordered lists of items -- that must satisfy multiple objectives beyond relevance, such as constraints defined over item attributes or fairness constraints. Existing multiobjective approaches either rely on post-processing techniques designed for non-generative settings, or incorporate auxiliary objectives directly into model training. The former does not explicitly account for the sequential nature of generative RS, while the latter is often impractical in large-scale systems.
  We propose a lightweight, inference-time decoding layer that augments autoregressive generative RS to support multiobjective slate generation without modifying or retraining the underlying model. We formulate decoding as an online constrained optimisation problem, where items are selected sequentially, and trade-offs between relevance and auxiliary objectives are adjusted dynamically based on the remaining constraint slack, i.e., how much of each objective remains to be satisfied. This is implemented via a stochastic primal-dual approximation scheme that balances relevance and auxiliary objectives during generation.
  We provide theoretical guarantees on constraint violation and regret, and evaluate the proposed approach through extensive offline experiments and a large-scale online A/B experiment in a real-world recommender system. Our results show consistent improvements in multiobjective trade-offs, including a +1.8\% gain in the auxiliary objectives achieved at zero cost to user satisfaction.

</details>


### [80] [LISA: Linear-Indexed Sparse Attention for Efficient Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19358)
*Yu Zhao,Zekun Zhang,Fan Jiang,Bo Zeng,Linlong Xu,Shimin Shan,Yu Liu,Longyue Wang,Weihua Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LISA（线性索引稀疏注意力），一种即插即用的注意力替换模块，将推理复杂度从O(n²)降至O(nM)，在16K上下文下实现50%推理加速，同时推理基准平均提升5.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链式推理（Long CoT）模型在测试时扩展范式下推理上下文长度不断增加，但标准自注意力的O(n²)计算复杂度导致推理成本急剧上升，严重限制了长CoT推理在生产环境中的部署。

Method: LISA包含两个并行组件：(1) 线性注意力模块（O(n)复杂度）提供长程记忆；(2) 闪电索引器（Lightning Indexer）从全上下文中选择Top-M重要 token 输入稀疏自注意力。两分支通过门控机制融合，复杂度降为O(nM)。采用两阶段训练：阶段1用知识蒸馏优化线性注意力+滑动窗口注意力；阶段2引入索引器替代滑动窗口，用逐头KL散度损失对齐教师模型的注意力模式。

Result: 在DeepSeek蒸馏Qwen模型上，16K上下文下实现50%推理加速，在AIME和MATH-500等推理基准上平均性能提升5.6%。

Conclusion: LISA通过线性注意力与稀疏索引注意力的混合设计，在不需从头预训练的情况下有效降低长序列推理成本，同时提升推理性能，为长CoT推理模型的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in long chain-of-thought reasoning models such as DeepSeek-R1 have led to increasingly longer inference context lengths under the test-time scaling paradigm. However, the O(n^2) computational complexity of standard self-attention causes inference costs to grow sharply with long sequences, limiting the deployment of long-CoT reasoning in production settings. To address this, we propose LISA (Linear-Indexed Sparse Attention), a plug-and-play attention replacement module that requires no pretraining from scratch. LISA integrates two lightweight components in parallel within the original model: (1) a Linear Attention module that provides long-range memory with O(n) time complexity; (2) a Lightning Indexer that selects the top-M important tokens from the full context to feed into a Sparse Self-Attention. The two branches are fused via a gating mechanism, reducing inference complexity from O(n^2) to O(nM) (M << n) for generating n tokens. We design a two-stage training pipeline: Stage 1 initializes the model by integrating the linear attention to capture long-range dependencies, complemented by a sliding-window attention mechanism that is optimized via knowledge distillation to approximate the full self-attention distribution of a frozen teacher model. In Stage 2, we further introduce the Indexer to replace the static sliding-window mechanism, enabling dynamic token selection from broader contexts. The Indexer is trained using a novel per-head KL divergence loss, which aligns its selection behavior with the attention patterns of the teacher model. Experiments on DeepSeek-distilled-Qwen models demonstrate that LISA achieves a 50% inference speedup under 16K-token context, while improving average performance by 5.6% on reasoning benchmarks including AIME and MATH-500.

</details>


### [81] [Profile-Graph Memory for LLM Agents: Implicit Cross-Entity Traversal through Narrative Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19359)
*Shengtong Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MemHop多跳记忆基准（1,000问题，1-5跳深度）和ProGraph双层记忆架构（轮廓扩展+压缩残差），在MemHop上达80.1%，在LoCoMo上超FullContext 11.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM记忆基准主要评估单跳回忆，缺乏对多跳关联能力的衡量，而跨会话交互的LLM代理需要长期记忆支持。

Method: ProGraph架构包含两层：(1) 轮廓扩展——基于子串匹配的实体名遍历，作为显式知识图谱构建的轻量替代；(2) 压缩残差——在每次轮廓更新时零额外API成本地共提取精确日期、数量及命名实体。

Result: 全网格消融实验显示：轮廓扩展驱动多跳推理（移除后MemHop下降22.6pp），压缩残差驱动精确召回（不共提取时LoCoMo下降8.6pp），交叉效应在3pp以内。ProGraph在MemHop上达80.1%，在LoCoMo上达78.4%（超FullContext 11.3pp），优于Mem0、A-Mem、HippoRAG和RAG。

Conclusion: 跨基准机制专门化的发现表明，多跳推理与精确召回需要不同的记忆组件；ProGraph通过双层设计同时优化了这两个维度。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for LLM agents that interact across sessions, yet current memory benchmarks primarily evaluate single-hop recall, leaving multi-hop association largely unmeasured. We make three contributions. First, we introduce MemHop, a multi-hop memory benchmark of 1,000 questions at hop depths 1-5 across 10 social-network scenarios, with per-hop evidence annotations. Second, we present Profile-Graph Memory (ProGraph), a two-layer memory architecture combining (i) profile expansion -- substring-matched traversal of entity names that naturally appear in LLM-written profile narratives, a minimal alternative to explicit knowledge-graph construction -- and (ii) compression residuals -- exact dates, quantities, and named items co-extracted with each profile update at zero extra API cost. Third, a full-grid ablation shows cross-benchmark mechanism specialization: profile expansion drives multi-hop reasoning (-22.6pp on MemHop when removed) while compression residuals drive precision recall (-8.6pp on LoCoMo when not co-extracted), with cross-effects under 3pp within a single architecture. ProGraph averages 80.1% on MemHop (matching the FullContext reference) and 78.4% on LoCoMo (exceeding FullContext by 11.3pp), outperforming Mem0, A-Mem, HippoRAG, and RAG on both. We release MemHop, ProGraph, and baseline implementations.

</details>


### [82] [Lifted Representation Hypothesis in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19360)
*Bumjin Park,Jaesik Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“提升表征假说”，认为LLM通过共享潜在结构而非孤立事实更新记忆，并通过异常学习实验发现LLM在嵌套规则下易出现“破碎化”失败，而“提升”则常过早发生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM常能从个别观测中归纳出通用规则，但其如何存储、选择和修订这些规则结构仍不清楚，因此需要提出系统性的假说来解释这一过程。

Method: 提出“提升表征假说”，将“提升”视为跨实例对称性的高效利用，“破碎化”视为对粗粒度结构的精细化细分。通过在上下文学习、LoRA和全量微调三种设置下进行受控的异常学习实验来验证。

Result: LLM在嵌套规则与异常数据下容易发生破碎化失败（即未能正确细分规则），同时提升常常过早发生（即过早泛化）。

Conclusion: 研究强调了需要深入理解LLM中数据与规则结构之间的关系，为改进LLM的规则学习与记忆更新机制提供了方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often answer queries by mapping individual observations to more general rule-like structures. However, it remains unclear how these structures are stored, selected, and revised. To study this process, we propose thelifted representation hypothesis: LLMs update memory through shared latent structures rather than isolated instance-level facts. This view frames lifting as an efficient use of symmetry across instances, and shattering as the refinement of coarse lifted structures into more specific subtypes. We evaluate LLMs' lifting and shattering through controlled exception-learning experiments across in-context learning, LoRA, and full fine-tuning. We find that LLMs are vulnerable to shattering failures when data are governed by nested rules and exceptions, while lifting often occurs prematurely. These results highlight the need to study the relation between data and rule structures in LLMs.

</details>


### [83] [GraphContainer: A Unified Platform for Comparing and Debugging Graph RAG Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19362)
*Seonho An,Chaejeong Hyun,Min-Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GraphContainer是一个统一和可视化不同图RAG工作流的平台，通过统一图表示层和图记录器，解决现有图RAG方法碎片化、不兼容及缺乏可视化工具的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图RAG方法高度碎片化、不兼容，不同框架的图格式存在结构异质性，且缺乏细粒度可视化工具，导致评估和比较检索行为极其困难。

Method: 提出GraphContainer平台，包含两个核心组件：(1) 统一图表示（UGR）层，用于标准化多格式图；(2) 图记录器（Graph Recorder），用于追踪并可视化逐步检索过程。

Result: 通过交互式Web界面，GraphContainer能够导入异构图，并对图RAG方法进行实时、可追踪的可视化调试，支持对不同图格式和检索策略进行受控比较。

Conclusion: GraphContainer降低了研究人员和实践者设计最优图RAG管线的门槛，实现了图RAG工作流的统一与可视化调试。

Abstract: Graph RAG mitigates hallucinations and stale knowledge in LLMs, particularly for multi-hop question answering. However, existing approaches remain highly fragmented and incompatible. The structural heterogeneity of graph formats across different frameworks and the lack of granular visualization tools make it exceedingly difficult to evaluate and compare retrieval behaviors. To bridge this gap, we propose GraphContainer, a novel platform designed to unify and visualize diverse graph RAG workflows. GraphContainer features two key components: (1) a Unified Graph Representation (UGR) layer that seamlessly standardizes multi-format graphs, and (2) a Graph Recorder that tracks and visually renders the step-by-step retrieval process. Through an interactive web interface, we demonstrate GraphContainer's ability to import heterogeneous graphs and perform live, traceable visual debugging of graph RAG methods. Ultimately, we show how GraphContainer enables controlled comparisons of various graph formats and retrieval strategies, lowering the barrier for researchers and practitioners to design optimal graph RAG pipelines. A demonstration video is available at https://youtu.be/O02eNJLwkU0.

</details>


### [84] [AdaRoPE: Not All Attention Heads Should Rotate and Scale Equally](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19363)
*Shaowen Wang,Yuke Zheng,Tansheng Zhu,Shuang Chen,Shaofan Liu,Suncong Zheng,Jian Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaRoPE，针对传统RoPE对所有注意力头使用统一频率和缩放的问题，为每个注意力头配备可学习的旋转频率和注意力缩放因子，在长上下文和长度泛化场景中取得更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RoPE对所有注意力头强制使用统一的频率调度和缩放，但不同功能的注意力头需要不同的频率范围和注意力缩放因子。这种忽略头部差异的做法导致嵌入维度利用不足，尤其在长上下文设置下性能下降。

Method: AdaRoPE为每个注意力头引入可学习的旋转频率和注意力缩放因子，使模型能够根据头部功能自适应调整位置编码参数。

Result: 预训练LLM使用AdaRoPE后持续优于现有RoPE变体（包括partial RoPE和NoPE基线）。在上下文扩展中，统一缩放（如YaRN）是次优的，而AdaRoPE的头部特定缩放在外推和长上下文继续预训练中都能更好地保留短上下文性能。

Conclusion: 优化旋转位置嵌入应在单个注意力头级别进行，头部特定的频率和缩放设置能显著提升模型的长上下文能力。

Abstract: Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) is widely adopted in Transformers to encode positional information, yet standard implementations enforce a uniform frequency schedule and scaling across all attention heads. Using simplified retrieval tasks and length generalization scenarios, we show -- both empirically and theoretically -- that heads with different functional roles require distinct frequency ranges and attention scaling factors to operate effectively. Ignoring this structure leads to suboptimal utilization of embedding dimensions and degraded performance, particularly under long-context settings. To address these limitations, we propose AdaRoPE, which equips each attention head with learnable rotation frequencies and attention scaling factors. Pretrained LLMs with AdaRoPE consistently outperform existing RoPE variants, including partial RoPE and NoPE baselines. For context extension, we further show that uniform frequency and attention scaling, used in methods such as YaRN, are suboptimal. By applying head-specific scaling, AdaRoPE enables better context extension while better preserving short-context performance in both the extrapolation setting and the long-context continued pretraining setting. These results highlight the importance of optimizing rotary position embedding at the level of individual attention heads.

</details>


### [85] [Statistically Grounded Sparse-Feature Interventions for Activation-Space Control in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19364)
*Oshayer Siddique,J. M Areeb Uzair Alam,Md Jobayer Rahman Rafy,Syed Rifat Raiyan,Hasan Mahmud,Md Kamrul Hasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于SAE特征的可解释激活引导流水线，通过六条件可靠性过滤、Borda共识排序和Cohen's d加权组合构建引导方向，实验发现引导效果高度局部化且原始属性移动与质量保持之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于SAE的激活引导方法要么依赖学习到的引导目标，要么使用单一标准的特征选择，缺乏透明性和可解释性。作者希望提出一种更透明、无需优化的引导方法。

Method: (1) 六条件可靠性过滤筛选SAE特征；(2) 使用未加权的Borda共识对F检验、KSG互信息和Cohen's d三个互补统计量进行排序；(3) 基于Cohen's d加权组合SAE解码器行构建引导方向，灵感来自Fisher-LDA，并假设SAE特征近似去相关。

Result: 在Gemma系列三个模型、四个行为域、356种层-强度配置上测试，最强配置下逻辑正确性引导在Gemma 2 9B上达到+1.16的主分数变化。但更重要的发现是：可用引导高度依赖于模型、域、层和强度的具体组合。

Conclusion: 激活引导评估应同时报告质量约束下的成功率和原始行为偏移。代码和数据已公开。

Abstract: Activation steering offers a lightweight alternative to fine-tuning for behavioral control of large language models, but SAE-based steering methods often rely on learned steering objectives or single-criterion feature selection. We introduce a transparent SAE-feature steering pipeline that first applies a six-condition reliability filter, then ranks sparse features through an unweighted Borda consensus over three complementary statistics: $F$-test, KSG mutual information, and Cohen's $d$. The resulting steering direction is constructed as a Cohen's-$d$-weighted combination of SAE decoder rows, providing an optimization-free direction motivated by Fisher-LDA under approximate SAE-feature decorrelation. Across three Gemma-family models, four behavioral domains, and 356 layer-strength configurations, the method produces measurable domain-specific shifts while revealing a substantial gap between raw attribute movement and quality-preserving generation. In the strongest configuration, logical-correctness steering reaches a primary-score delta of $+1.16$ in Gemma~2 9B; however, our broader finding is that usable steering is highly localized by model, domain, layer, and strength. These results argue that activation-steering evaluations should report quality-conditioned success alongside raw behavioral shift. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/Oshayer-Siddique/LLM-Steering-Using-SAE.

</details>


### [86] [Logic-Guided Data Extraction with Answer Set Programming and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19365)
*Mario Alviano,Lorenzo Grillo,Nicola Leone,Fabrizio Lo Scudo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种逻辑引导的数据抽取框架，将大语言模型（LLM）与回答集编程（ASP）结合。LLM生成候选事实，ASP负责验证、推理、一致性检查和控制。通过交错执行LLM调用与ASP推导，框架能推理出逻辑隐含的事实并及早发现不一致性。实验表明，该方法减少了LLM调用次数，提升了抽取质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在语义数据抽取中虽能生成候选关系事实，但在需要复杂组合推理和全局一致性的任务上不可靠，现有独立查询所有目标谓词的方法效率低且易产生虚假输出。

Method: 提出LLM+ASP的交替框架：LLM生成候选事实，ASP进行逻辑验证、推理和一致性检查；通过ASP推理识别每个阶段逻辑上可接受的谓词来引导抽取查询，并利用缓存机制减少求解器调用。

Result: 理论证明该框架在温和假设下与基线方法在最终事实集上等价，但需要更少的LLM调用。在ASP衍生基准上实验表明，框架减少了LLM调用次数，并通过抑制虚假输出提升了抽取质量。

Conclusion: 非单调逻辑编程（ASP）能有效控制LLM的语义抽取过程，提升效率与准确性，展示了逻辑约束在大模型应用中的价值。

Abstract: When Large Language Models (LLMs) are used for semantic data extraction from unstructured text, producing candidate relational facts from natural language, they may remain unreliable for tasks requiring complex combinatorial reasoning and global consistency. This paper proposes a logic-guided data extraction framework combining LLM-based extraction with Answer Set Programming (ASP). The LLM produces candidate facts, whereas ASP performs validation, inference, consistency checking, and control. Unlike existing pipelines that query the LLM independently for all target predicates, the proposed approach uses ASP reasoning to identify which predicates are logically admissible at each stage and to guide extraction queries. By interleaving LLM calls with ASP derivation, the framework infers logically implied facts without further extraction and detects inconsistencies early. We formalize the pipeline and prove that, under mild assumptions, it is equivalent to the baseline approach with respect to the final extracted facts, while requiring fewer LLM calls. We also introduce a caching mechanism for logic-based control queries, exploiting monotonicity of conjunctive queries over incrementally constructed fact sets to reduce solver invocations. Experiments on ASP-derived benchmarks show that the framework reduces LLM calls and improves extraction quality by mitigating spurious outputs, demonstrating the value of non-monotonic logic programming for controlled semantic extraction.

</details>


### [87] [Geometry-Guided Constraint Learning for LLM Safety Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19366)
*Fumiaki Uehara,Koo Imai,Masato Tsutsumi,Keigo Kansa,Sora Usui,Yuki Kobiyama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SaP方法学习LLM隐藏空间中的线性半空间约束，发现SAE特征提取后K=2对12/14类别最优，准确率达96-99%，并引入具有可学习孔径的锥约束，通过三阶段训练实现稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决SaP方法中需要为每个类别手动调整约束数量K的问题，减少穷举搜索的负担。

Method: 利用稀疏自编码器(SAE)提取特征，使K=2成为最优；引入锥约束(cone constraint)替代平面约束，其孔径可学习以适应不同类别的聚类集中度；采用三阶段训练策略保证稳定性。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-9B模型上，12/14类别在K=2时达到最优，每类别准确率96-99%，无需K=4-25的穷举搜索。

Conclusion: SAE特征空间中的安全边界具有低维线性描述，与线性表示假说一致；锥约束能更好地适应不同类别数据的几何特性。

Abstract: Safety as Polytope (SaP) learns linear half-space constraints in LLM hidden space but requires per-category tuning of the constraint count K. We show that sparse autoencoder (SAE) feature extraction resolves this: K=2 becomes optimal for 12/14 categories on Qwen3.5-9B, achieving 96-99% accuracy per category on our BeaverTails classification benchmark, largely eliminating the need for exhaustive sweeps (K=4-25 with random initialization). This convergence to two planes is consistent with the Linear Representation Hypothesis, providing suggestive evidence that safety boundaries in this setting admit a low-dimensional linear description in the SAE feature space. Building on this geometric perspective, we introduce a cone constraint whose learnable aperture adapts to each category's cluster concentration, stabilized by a three-phase training

</details>


### [88] [Rethinking Uncertainty Evaluation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19367)
*Krish Matta,Atharv Naphade,Andy Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出校准（calibration）不足以评估LLM置信度，提出C1指标从结构一致性、忠实性和有用性三个维度衡量置信度估计的合理性，并发现主流模型普遍违反这些条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 校准作为评估LLM置信度的主要标准存在不足：它无法排除琐碎不一致的估计、依赖评估分布、且不能检验置信度是否能解释为一致的概率函数。因此需要更严格的评估框架。

Method: 将概率信念一致性形式化为三个维度：结构一致性（structural coherence）、忠实性（faithfulness）和有用性（usefulness），并操作化为C1指标进行量化评估。

Result: 主流LLM置信度估计虽然在校准上表现良好，但系统性违反三个维度的条件：模型在31%的情况下对逻辑上更简单的问题赋予更低置信度；常见干预（如RLHF、思维链）降低了RMSCE但未改变结构性违规；RLHF和思维链提升了有用性指标却未能恢复一致性。

Conclusion: 当前LLM置信度估计不能被解释为一致的概率；C1框架为此提供了测量和弥合差距的工具。

Abstract: Calibration is the primary criterion for evaluating LLM confidence, but it is insufficient: it admits trivially incoherent estimators, depends on the evaluation distribution, and does not test the extent to which the estimation can be interpreted as a consistent, underlying probability function. What we actually need is for LLM confidence estimates to satisfy the conditions required of coherent probabilistic beliefs. We formalize these conditions along three axes (structural coherence, faithfulness, and usefulness) and operationalize them as the C1 metrics. Widely used estimators systematically violate these conditions despite appearing well-calibrated: models assign lower confidence to logically easier questions 31\% of the time, and common interventions reducing RMSCE leave structural violations unchanged, suggesting that calibration is orthogonal to probabilistic validity. RLHF and chain-of-thought improve usefulness metrics without restoring coherence. Our results show current LLM confidence estimates cannot be interpreted as coherent probabilities; our framework provides the tools to measure and close this gap.

</details>


### [89] [Spectral-LSH: Sub-Quadratic Prompt Compression via Krylov-Projected Locality-Sensitive Hashing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19368)
*Ali Mahdavi,Azaseh Zamanifar,Amirfarhad Farhadi,Omid Kashefi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Spectral-LSH，一种无需训练的提示压缩方法，利用Krylov子空间方法近似注意力核的 dominant 成分，结合SimHash在注意力特征空间中对相似token进行聚类聚合为宏观token。实验表明存在压缩率相变阈值，自适应后端可在低压缩率下使用分块、高压缩率下使用谱聚类来平衡质量与延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长提示推理中，预填充阶段的注意力计算随序列长度呈二次缩放，导致计算开销巨大。现有方法要么需要额外训练，要么压缩效果不佳，亟需一种无需训练且高效的提示压缩方法。

Method: 使用Krylov子空间方法结合随机特征近似注意力核的 dominant 成分，避免显式的O(N²)注意力核计算；然后在获得的注意力特征空间中应用SimHash对相似token进行聚类，并将其聚合为带有因果位置分配的宏观token（macro-tokens）。

Result: 在C4数据集上，Mistral-7B、Qwen2.5-7B和Qwen2.5-14B的实验显示压缩率存在相变：ρ<4×时分块最优，ρ>8×时谱方法保持质量。在ρ=16×时，Qwen2.5-7B将PPL比从353.409降至196.963，Qwen2.5-14B从9.533降至3.427。在结构化长上下文压力测试中，局部LSH在8×压缩时全面优于分块。

Conclusion: Spectral-LSH在无需训练的条件下实现了有效的长提示压缩，发现了压缩率与token冗余之间的相变关系。自适应后端能根据压缩率切换策略，兼顾质量和效率，但分块在延迟上仍具优势。

Abstract: Long-prompt inference remains expensive because prefill attention scales quadratically with sequence length. We propose Spectral-LSH, a training-free prompt compression method that operates before the prompt enters the language model. Spectral-LSH approximates the dominant components of an implicit attention-kernel operator using a Krylov subspace method together with random features, avoiding explicit $O(N^2)$ attention-kernel materialization. It then applies SimHash in the resulting attention eigenspace to group similar tokens and aggregate them into macro-tokens with causal positional assignments.
  We evaluate Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3, Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, and Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct on C4. Our experiments reveal a compression-ratio phase transition. Below $ρ= 4 \times$, local token redundancy is low enough that lightweight chunking typically provides the best latency--quality trade-off. Above $ρ= 8 \times$, the spectral path preserves quality that chunking loses. At $ρ= 16 \times$, Qwen2.5-7B (adaptive) reduces the PPL ratio from 353.409 to 196.963, while Qwen2.5-14B (adaptive) reduces it from 9.533 to 3.427.
  On a small long-context structured stress test containing JSON-like, code-like, and table-like inputs, local LSH also improves every metric over chunking at $8 \times$. The adaptive backend captures both regimes by using the chunk path at low compression and spectral clustering at high compression, although chunking remains the fastest backend in total latency.

</details>


### [90] [Beyond Tracking or Shortcut: Composition-Bounded Predictive States in Poker Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19369)
*Quanhao Li,Qianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过限定荷官扑克模型实证发现，模型隐藏状态中可恢复的对手范围信息主要来自可见的下注组成，而非真正的信念追踪，并提出"组成边界预测支持"作为解释框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究使用隐藏状态探针从序列模型中恢复潜在标签，但这并不证明模型维持了关于隐藏状态的后验信念分布。本文旨在澄清这一歧义，实证检验模型是否真正进行信念追踪。

Method: 在无限注限注扑克自回归模型上，训练对手范围探针，并通过控制动作/价值、公共下注组成等变量，比较隐藏状态与可见信息对预测能力的贡献。

Result: 可见的公共下注组成解释了大部分对手范围信号，剩余隐藏状态探针准确率显著下降；在匹配组成对比下，所有种子均呈负向结果。

Conclusion: 隐藏状态中恢复的信息主要来自可见组成而非残差隐藏结构，正向信念探针需经针对性替代解释验证，不能直接视为信念追踪的证据。

Abstract: Hidden-state probes often recover latent labels in imperfect-information sequence models, but this alone does not establish that a model maintains a posterior belief distribution over hidden states. This paper studies this ambiguity in a no-range Limit Hold'em autoregressive model trained only on action and value targets, not on an opponent's hand or range. Opponent-range probes are positive after action/value controls in two of three seeds, and the behavior head predicts held-out actions about five percentage points above a baseline using only observable public history. However, visible public betting composition explains more opponent-range signal than residual hidden states, suggesting that most recoverable information comes from betting summaries. Action/value+composition baselines reach 16.5-16.7% top-10 accuracy while composition-residual hidden probes fall to 11.4-12.2%, and matched-composition comparisons are negative in every seed. We call this evidence pattern composition-bounded predictive support: hidden states remain behavior-predictive and opponent-range correlated, but most recoverable range information is explained by visible betting composition rather than residual hidden-state structure. This is a case-study claim about opponent-range representational evidence, not exact Bayesian posterior tracking or a causal belief mechanism. Synthetic control and oracle validations show that the same diagnostics accept posterior-sensitive states and reject raw composition states under matched controls. Thus positive belief probes should be interpreted through targeted alternatives before being treated as evidence of belief tracking.

</details>


### [91] [Mitigating Scaffolding Collapse in Socratic Tutors via Representation Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19371)
*Jing Shao,Qifeng Wu,Hanyu Zhang,Sixia Sun,Jun Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对基于LLM的苏格拉底式辅导中出现的"脚手架坍塌"问题（即辅导者逐渐放弃引导式提问而直接给出答案），提出了脚手架保持表示对齐（Scaffold-Preserving Representation Alignment）框架，通过两阶段训练（监督微调+轨迹加权偏好优化与边界保持表示损失）来维持隐藏状态的分离性，在五个STEM学科和五种红队攻击策略下取得更低的坍塌率（32%）和更晚的坍塌 onset。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-based苏格拉底式辅导在多轮对话中会因持续的来自学生的压力而逐渐放弃引导式提问，直接揭示解决方案（即坍塌）。现有防御方法只约束可观测的响应层面，忽视了导致轨迹级坍塌的隐藏表征漂移问题。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段通过监督微调进行热身；第二阶段结合轨迹加权直接偏好优化（trajectory-weighted DPO）与边界保持表示损失（margin-preserving representation loss），锚定冻结的参考状态，维持"脚手架保持"与"坍塌诱导"两种隐藏状态之间的分离。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上，在五个STEM学科和五种红队攻击策略下，将坍塌率降至32%，平均坍塌 onset 延迟至9轮以上，同时保持较低的过度拒绝率。

Conclusion: 表征层面的对齐能够提升长时域苏格拉底式辅导在红队攻击协议下的鲁棒性，为应对隐藏表征漂移导致的对话轨迹坍塌提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based Socratic tutors increasingly guide students through multi-turn questioning, but they can suffer from scaffolding collapse: under sustained student pressure, a tutor gradually abandons guided inquiry and reveals solutions directly. Prior defenses primarily constrain observable responses through prompting, preference optimization, or filtering, leaving the internal representation drift that precedes trajectory-level collapse largely unaddressed. We propose Scaffold-Preserving Representation Alignment, a two-stage framework that first warms up a Socratic tutor with supervised fine-tuning, then combines trajectory-weighted direct preference optimization with a margin-preserving representation loss anchored to frozen reference states. Our method is designed to maintain separation between scaffold-preserving and collapse-inducing hidden states across dialogue turns. We evaluate our method across five STEM disciplines and five red-teaming attack strategies. On Qwen3-8B, our method lowers Collapse Rate to 32%, delays average collapse onset beyond nine turns, and keeps over-refusal low, suggesting that representation-level alignment can improve the robustness of long-horizon Socratic tutoring under our red-teaming protocol.

</details>


### [92] [Euclean: Automated Geometry Problem Formalization with Unified Verification in Lean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19374)
*Linbin Tang,Jingyan You,Zilin Kang,Hanzhang Liu,Sophia Zhang,Zenan Li,Chenrui Cao,Liangcheng Song,Jiaao Wu,Xian Zhang,Fan Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Euclean框架，用于将几何问题自动形式化为原生Mathlib代码，并构建了OMNI-Geometry和Numina-Geometry两个大型数据集。训练Goedel v2模型后，证明成功率从13.6%提升至15.1%，验证了数据集质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有形式推理系统存在割裂：代数和数论在Lean中处理，而几何仍依赖专用语言，缺乏形式化保证。已有的几何形式化工作（LeanEuclid, LeanGeo）使用自定义公理系统，与标准Mathlib不兼容，且数据集规模小（<1,100道题），限制了大模型训练。因此需要一种能原生形式化几何到Mathlib的方法。

Method: 提出Euclean四阶段框架：(1) 约束显式化（Constraint Explication）；(2) 配置锚定（Configuration Anchoring）；(3) 形式化映射（Formalization Mapping）；(4) 迭代修复（Iterative Repair）。用于将几何问题自动转化为原生Mathlib代码。

Result: 构建了OMNI-Geometry（768道竞赛题）和Numina-Geometry（177,597道题），为最大规模几何形式化数据集。人工评估TOP1准确率48.89%，TOP5准确率73.33%。使用该形式化数据训练的Goedel v2模型，证明成功率从13.6%提升至15.1%。

Conclusion: Euclean框架有效弥合了几何形式化与Mathlib之间的鸿沟，解决了隐式图解假设等挑战。大规模高质量数据集验证了对统一神经定理证明的促进作用。

Abstract: Recent formal reasoning systems have reached IMO-level performance, yet they leave a fragmented landscape: algebra and number theory are handled in Lean, while geometry still relies on domain-specific languages with limited formal guarantees. This split increases the trusted computing base and hinders unified model development. Existing geometry-in-Lean efforts (LeanEuclid, LeanGeo) introduce custom axiom systems incompatible with standard Mathlib, and their small scale ($<$ 1,100 problems) limits large-scale training. Native Mathlib autoformalization of geometry, however, poses distinct challenges: implicit diagrammatic assumptions (e.g., topological configuration and non-degeneracy) must be made explicit rather than deferred to external solvers, and models must adapt to Mathlib's small, rapidly evolving geometry infrastructure. We present Euclean, a four-stage framework - constraint explication, configuration anchoring, formalization mapping, and iterative repair - for automatically formalizing geometry in native Mathlib. We construct OMNI-Geometry (768 competition problems) and Numina-Geometry (177,597 problems), the largest geometry formalization dataset in Lean. Human evaluation shows 48.89% TOP1 and 73.33% TOP5 accuracy. Training Goedel v2 on our formalizations improves proof success from 13.6% to 15.1%, validating dataset quality for unified neural theorem proving. Code and datasets: https://github.com/tlb-22/Euclean.

</details>


### [93] [CrackedPDFs: A Controlled Benchmark for Hidden Prompt Injection in PDFs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19396)
*Pukaphol Thienpreecha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CrackedPDFs基准测试集，用于检测PDF中的隐藏提示注入。通过评估多种检测方法，发现结合文档结构的混合检测器效果最佳(F1=0.96)，而纯文本模型在严格测试下存在脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文档型LLM系统在处理PDF时通常将其扁平化为纯文本，这一过程可能丢失关键证据，导致无法判断指令是否实际对用户可见。因此需要专门的基准来检测PDF中的隐藏提示注入问题。

Method: 构建了包含29,322个PDF的大规模基准数据集（来自4,983个基础文档，含9,774个注入文件和19,548个良性/混淆文件），采用留出来源划分、配对良性-混淆控制、标签洗牌检查和捷径审计等严格评估方法，对比了PromptGuard、规则基线、纯结构学习模型和混合检测器。

Result: 混合检测器在2,919文档测试集上F1=0.96，ROC-AUC=0.998，PR-AUC=0.997，95.9%的配对中将注入文件排在良性混淆文件之上。PromptGuard在仅提取文本时召回率低，纯结构模型在配对控制下表现弱，TF-IDF文本模型虽完美但未通过捷径审计。

Conclusion: 文档感知的混合检测在受控配对评估中有用，但尚不能证明广泛的现实世界鲁棒性或可靠的跨系列泛化能力。

Abstract: Document-based LLM systems often flatten a PDF before guardrails inspect it. That step can discard evidence that an instruction was never visible to the user. We introduce CrackedPDFs, a controlled benchmark for hidden prompt injection in PDFs. The benchmark contains 29,322 generated PDFs from 4,983 base docu ments. It includes 9,774 injected files and 19,548 benign or matched-confounder files. We evaluate PromptGuard and a rule baseline. We also evaluate structural only learned models and a sanitized hybrid detector. The evaluation uses held-out provenance splits and paired benign-confounder controls. It also uses label-shuffle checks and shortcut audits. On a 2,919-document held-out test set, the hybrid de tector reaches 0.960 F1. ROC-AUC is 0.998 and PR-AUC is 0.997. It also ranks injected files above matched benign confounders in 95.9% of 973 pairs. Prompt Guard has low recall when given extracted text only. Structural-only learned mod els are weak under paired controls. A text-only TF-IDF model reaches perfect held-out scores but fails shortcut audits. These results show that document-aware hybrid detection is useful under controlled paired evaluation. They do not show broad real-world robustness or reliable cross-family generalization.

</details>


### [94] [HyGRL: Adaptive Hybrid Graph Reasoning for Multi-Entity Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19398)
*Junyi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HyGRL框架，通过将非结构化文本嵌入知识图谱构建异构网络，结合模仿学习和强化学习的二阶段训练，实现多实体组合问题的灵活证据检索，在保持低成本和近实时推理的同时，在答案准确性和推理忠实度上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在困境：标准RAG缺乏动态推理能力，传统图RAG受限于结构稀疏性，而LLM构建的图RAG成本过高。需要一种统一框架来融合文本丰富性与结构化知识。

Method: 提出HyGRL统一框架：将非结构化文本嵌入结构化知识图谱形成异构网络；将推理建模为自适应结构归纳；采用两阶段训练：先通过模仿学习蒸馏启发式专家信号，再通过强化学习利用LLM驱动的偏好奖励优化策略。

Result: 在答案准确性和推理忠实度上超越SOTA基线，同时保持极低的token消耗和近实时推理速度。

Conclusion: HyGRL有效融合了文本丰富性与结构化知识，为多实体组合问题提供了一种低成本、高效率的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-entity compositional questions pose significant challenges to existing retrieval-augmented language models. Conventional methods fall into a dilemma: standard RAG lacks dynamic reasoning, traditional Graph-RAG is limited by structural sparsity, and LLM-constructed Graph-RAG incurs prohibitive costs. We propose \textbf{\fwa}, a unified framework that embeds unstructured text into structured knowledge graphs, creating a heterogeneous network for flexible evidence retrieval. Reasoning is formulated as adaptive structure induction, learned via a robust two-stage process: (1) imitation learning distills heuristic expert signals, and (2) reinforcement learning refines the policy using LLM-driven preference rewards. Experiments demonstrate that {\fwa} effectively merges textual richness with structural knowledge, outperforming SOTA baselines in answer accuracy and reasoning fidelity while maintaining extremely low token costs and near real-time inference((code available at https://github.com/wjywjy123/HyGRL) .

</details>


### [95] [ITPEval: Benchmarking Formal Translation Across Interactive Theorem Provers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19407)
*Jiayi Wu,Robert Joseph George,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ITPEval，首个跨四大交互式定理证明器（Lean 4、Rocq、Isabelle、HOL Light）的自动化形式证明翻译基准，包含1,560个源文件和6,848条定理，评估发现语句翻译最高29.1% pass@1，证明翻译仅10.5%，库不匹配是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式定理证明的生态系统碎片化严重，证明被隔离在不同非兼容系统中，限制了基于学习的证明器的训练数据以及验证结果的可移植性。

Method: 构建ITPEval基准，包含控制层（公理化文件隔离翻译难度）和生态层（真实库暴露API与证明风格差异）；开发itpeval统一验证基础设施，提供状态隔离的温后端以保留原生检查语义；在12个有向翻译对上评估5个前沿/开源LLM的语句和证明翻译能力。

Result: 语句翻译最高29.1% pass@1，证明翻译最高10.5%；控制层定理证明翻译达29.7% pass@1，生态层仅5.2%；确定性Lean 4 BEq检查显示仅54.0%的已验证翻译具有语义等价性；多ITP上下文将Lean 4成功率从4.8%提升至10.6%。

Conclusion: 库不匹配是跨ITP翻译的主要瓶颈；原生类型检查会高估语义保真度；Rocq和HOL Light比Lean 4和Isabelle更适合作为形式化目标；多ITP上下文有助于提升翻译性能。基准、验证基础设施和评估管线均已公开。

Abstract: Formal theorem proving has emerged as a frontier challenge for machine learning, yet the ecosystem is fragmented: proofs remain siloed across incompatible systems, limiting both training data for learning-based provers and the portability of verified results. We present ITPEval, the first benchmark for evaluating automated formal proof translation across four major ITPs (Lean 4, Rocq, Isabelle, and HOL Light), spanning two distinct logical foundations. Our benchmark comprises 1,560 source files and 6,848 theorems organized into a controlled tier of axiomatized files that isolates foundational translation difficulty, and an ecosystem tier drawn from real libraries that exposes API and proof-style mismatches. We release itpeval, a unified multi-ITP verification infrastructure with state-isolated warm backends that preserve per-artifact native checking semantics. We evaluate both statement and proof translation across five frontier and open-weight LLMs on 12 directed translation pairs: statement translation peaks at 29.1% pass@1 and proof translation at 10.5%; controlled theorems reach 29.7% proof pass@1 versus 5.2% for ecosystem-level translations, confirming that library mismatch is the dominant bottleneck. In addition to pass@k evaluation, a deterministic Lean 4 BEq check establishes equivalence for 54.0% of verified source-to-Lean 4 miniF2F statement translations, showing that native type-checking alone can substantially overestimate semantic fidelity; in an autoformalization/auto-informalization round-trip study, Rocq and HOL Light are easier formalization targets than Lean 4 and Isabelle, while multi-ITP context improves pooled Lean 4 success from 4.8% to 10.6%. Our benchmark, verification infrastructure, and evaluation pipelines are publicly released.

</details>


### [96] [FORCE-Bench: A Benchmark, Dataset, and Evaluation Harness for Agentic AI in Enterprise Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19409)
*Wolfgang M. Pauli,Sarah Panda,Kidus Admassu,Said Bleik,Ademola Okerinde,Jeremy Reynolds*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FORCE-Bench是一个面向运营金融领域的基准测试，包含251个专家标注的查询，从8个维度评估智能体系统。测试发现通用智能体系统在运营约束下表现不稳定，而专为金融领域构建的智能体更可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试强调通用能力、指令遵循或安全性，但很少直接评估智能体系统在运营金融工作流中的实际表现。金融专业人士需要智能体不仅提供准确、有依据的信息，还要确保信息可验证并始终遵守领域规则和约束。

Method: 构建FORCE-Bench基准，包含251个专家标注查询，采用基于评分标准的评估框架，涵盖准确性、引用、清晰度、深度、基础性、时效性、相关性和结构8个维度。在工具访问和延迟限制的部署条件下评估三类任务：金融义务研究、金融实体绩效研究和商业简报生成。

Result: 通用智能体系统在运营约束下无法持续满足金融领域质量要求，而专为金融打造的Finance Agent for Microsoft 365 Copilot在各维度上更可靠。

Conclusion: 通用智能体在运营金融领域的表现不够稳定，专用金融智能体更具优势。数据集、评分标准、测试框架和分析代码将开源，以支持可重复比较和适配其他企业金融环境。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have accelerated deployment of agentic systems in operational finance. Existing benchmarks emphasize measuring general capabilities, instruction following, or safety, but few directly address the operational finance workflows that agentic systems are now being deployed to automate. Finance professionals require agents to not only provide factually sound and properly grounded information, but also ensure that this information is verifiable and consistently adheres to rules and constraints of the operational finance domain. We introduce FORCE-Bench, which contains 251 expert-annotated queries and evaluates responses using a rubric-based framework calibrated to the requirements of the operational finance domain, across eight dimensions: accuracy, citations, clarity, depth, groundedness, recency, relevance, and structure. FORCE-Bench assesses agentic systems on three task types: financial obligation research (querying ERP systems for accounts receivable and payable data), financial entity performance research (answering time-bound questions from public filings and market data), and business brief generation (synthesising multi-source company intelligence reports). To reflect real deployment conditions, we evaluate our purpose-built agent, as well as the general-purpose agentic systems, under common tool access and latency-bounded settings. Results show that general-purpose agentic systems do not consistently meet finance-domain quality requirements under operational constraints, while the purpose-built Finance Agent for Microsoft 365 Copilot is more reliable across dimensions. We release the dataset, rubrics, harness, and analysis code as open-source to support reproducible comparison and adaptation to other enterprise finance environments.

</details>


### [97] [The Chronos Vulnerability: A Taxonomy of Temporal Persistence and Memory-Based Deception in Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19433)
*Om Narayan,Ramkinker Singh,Praveen Baskar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI从无状态生成模型向有状态自主智能体转变过程中出现的新型安全威胁——Chronos漏洞，包括内存注入攻击（MINJA）和休眠代理攻击，并提出了一套纵深防御框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统端点内容过滤器无法有效防御有状态架构下的持久化攻击，需要形式化威胁模型并构建新的防御体系。

Method: 形式化定义了基于持久化的攻击威胁模型和动态盲点（Dynamics Blindness）概念，在World of Workflows基准上验证攻击有效性，并综合分析了多种防御框架（AgentDoG、Agent-C、A-MemGuard、GPU TEE和零信任内存架构）。

Result: 证明了传统内容过滤器在有状态架构下不足，提出了纵深防御景观并分类了多种新兴防御框架。

Conclusion: AI自主智能体的有状态架构引入了一种隐蔽且延迟显现的安全威胁，需要多层次、硬件锚定的防御策略。

Abstract: The transition from stateless generative models in artificial intelligence to stateful, autonomous agents represents an architectural evolution that, while providing the capabilities of long-term planning and the automation of enterprise workflows, also represents the introduction of a new form of security threat, the Chronos Vulnerability. The Chronos Vulnerability represents the threat of memory-based attacks, including the Memory Injection Attack (MINJA) and the sleeper agent, in which the internal belief system of the autonomous agent is compromised, effectively decoupling the attack vector from the final catastrophic event. This study formalizes the threat model for persistence-based attacks and the threat of Dynamics Blindness in the context of the World of Workflows benchmark, demonstrating that traditional endpoint content filters are insufficient for the current stateful architecture. Consequently, this study synthesizes a defense-in-depth landscape, categorizing emerging frameworks such as diagnostic trajectory guardrails (AgentDoG), formal temporal verification (Agent-C), immunological memory consensus (A-MemGuard), and hardware-anchored trust via GPU-based Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) and Zero-Trust memory architectures.

</details>


### [98] [Sophisticated Policies from Epistemic Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19518)
*Wouter W. L. Nuijten,Bert de Vries*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证知性推理的核心优势并非树搜索本身，而在于其闭环控制结构：未来行动依赖未来状态。通过将这种结构纳入认知先验变分自由能框架，在反应迷宫基准上证明，只有同时具备认知驱动力和闭环推理的方法才能有效完成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清Sophisticated Inference的计算本质，探究其优势究竟来自树搜索还是更基本的闭环控制结构，并展示该结构可被认知先验变分推理所表征。

Method: 将Sophisticated Inference分解为认知先验变分自由能框架下的闭环控制结构，提出full-joint epistemic-prior active inference。在Reactivity Maze随机基准上比较五种变分目标：三种相同后验族的目标、动作-状态因子化目标、Sophisticated Inference和标准EFE规划。

Result: 缺乏认知成分的方法无法主动获取信息；不允许未来行动依赖未来状态的方法无法将信息转化为可靠的目标达成。只有Sophisticated Inference和full-joint认知先验主动推理能解决环境，两者兼具认知驱动力和闭环推理。

Conclusion: Sophisticated Inference的优势不在于树搜索本身，而在于其闭环形式的主动推理。这种闭环结构可通过认知先验变分推理实现，前提是后验保持未来行动对未来状态的依赖关系。

Abstract: Sophisticated Inference is a variant of active inference often associated with recursive belief modeling and tree search. We argue that its central computational role is simpler: within a planning horizon, it makes active inference closed-loop by allowing future actions to depend on future states and observations. This closed-loop structure can be represented in the epistemic-prior variational free energy framework. Epistemic priors supply the active-inference objective, while a joint posterior over future states and actions supplies the state-contingent control structure. We evaluate this decomposition in the Reactivity Maze, a stochastic benchmark designed to separate epistemic incentive from inner-horizon closed-loop control. The comparison includes three variational objectives with the same state-action posterior family, an action-state factorized active inference objective, Sophisticated Inference, and standard Expected Free Energy planning. The results show that neither ingredient is sufficient on its own. Methods without an epistemic component do not seek information, while methods that prevent future actions from depending on future states cannot turn information into reliable goal-reaching. By contrast, both Sophisticated Inference and full-joint epistemic-prior active inference solve the environment by combining epistemic drive with closed-loop inference. These results show that the advantage associated with Sophisticated Inference need not be specific to tree search itself. It arises from the closed-loop form of active inference, and this form can be represented in epistemic-prior variational inference when the posterior keeps future actions dependent on future states.

</details>


### [99] [Knowledge-Centric Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19592)
*Xuefei Julie Wang,Lauren Hyoseo Yoon,Chengrui Qu,Amanda Zichang Wang,Atharva Sehgal,Eric Mazumdar,Yisong Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出知识中心型自我改进范式（KSI），替代传统以智能体为中心的自我改进方法。通过让通用可弃置的智能体在执行任务后，向共享知识库贡献基于证据的见解，并经过论坛讨论和知识蒸馏实现改进。实验表明，该方法在抽象推理、编程等任务上提升解决率并降低成本，蒸馏知识可跨任务和跨LLM家族迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统以智能体为中心的自我改进方法（优化提示词、工作流或代码）存在维护成本高、难以迁移等局限：改进与特定智能体设计、任务分布或适应运行紧密绑定。作者希望探索一种互补范式，将改进固化在持久化的知识库中，而非智能体本身，以提升可审查性、可迁移性和可移植性。

Method: 提出知识中心型自我改进协议：1）智能体执行单个任务后，通过任务级和跨任务论坛贡献基于证据的见解；2）通过知识蒸馏将见解凝练为共享知识库；3）后续任务中智能体利用该知识库。智能体保持通用且可弃置，知识库作为持久化改进载体。

Result: 在抽象推理、编程和终端基准测试中，KSI协议相比以智能体为中心的基线方法，在提升解决率的同时降低了美元成本。蒸馏所得知识不仅能迁移到保留任务上，还能跨不同LLM家族（如不同模型）通用，证明改进不是LLM或运行特定的行为。

Conclusion: 自我改进的智能体系统可以主要由持久化的策管知识驱动进步，而非智能体本身。这为构建更可审查、可迁移和成本高效的AI系统提供了新视角。

Abstract: Self-improving AI systems typically treat the agent as the object that improves, by optimizing prompts, workflows, harnesses, or even the agent's own code. This agent-centric view can make improvements expensive to maintain and difficult to transfer, because gains become tied to a particular agent design, task distribution, or adaptation run. We study a complementary paradigm: knowledge-centric self-improvement, in which agents remain generic and disposable while the persistent object is a curated knowledge base that agents can leverage for future tasks. We conduct controlled case studies to operationalize this idea via a simple protocol. Agents attempt one task, then contribute evidence-grounded insights to a shared knowledge base via task-level and cross-task forums, followed by knowledge distillation. Because self-improvement is contained in the knowledge rather than the agent, improvement can be more inspectable, transferable, and portable. Across abstract reasoning, coding, and terminal benchmarks, this protocol improves solve rates while reducing dollar cost relative to agent-centric baselines. The resulting distilled knowledge also transfers to held-out tasks and across LLM families, indicating that the improvement is not merely an LLM- or run-specific behavior. These results support a new view of self-improving agentic systems: progress can be driven primarily by the curated persistent knowledge. Code is available at https://github.com/recursive-knowledge/KSI.

</details>


### [100] [Edge Intelligence in Civil Aviation: Paradigms, Techniques, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19676)
*Wenbin Li,Zhongtian Liao,Bolin Liu,Yongjie Zhou,Jingling Wu,Xiaoyong Lin,Jing Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了面向民用航空的边缘智能技术，分析了云中心化AI在延迟、离线能力与数据隐私方面的局限，提出了通过边缘推理、边缘学习及组织计算范式来构建低延迟、隐私保护且高韧性的航空AI服务方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 民用航空是安全关键领域，其运营产生海量异构边缘数据。传统云中心化AI部署存在高任务延迟、通信中断时缺乏离线能力、敏感数据集中化带来隐私与主权风险等问题，亟需将AI推向网络边缘。

Method: 采用全景综述方法：首先梳理边缘AI的运营动机，随后回顾边缘推理与边缘学习的关键技术（压缩、协同推理、分割学习等），接着介绍组织计算范式及在民航环境中的具体配置，最后展望新兴应用与未来趋势。

Result: 明确了边缘AI可通过压缩、协同推理和分割学习等技术，将感知、预测与决策逻辑靠近数据源，从而降低延迟、减少带宽占用、提升隐私保护，并在断连时维持优雅运行。

Conclusion: 精细化边缘解决方案能够补充云基础架构，为民用航空全生命周期提供低延迟、隐私保护且高韧性的AI服务。

Abstract: Civil aviation is safety critical and its operations, from flight decks and towers to ramps and maintenance, generate massive, heterogeneous data at the network edge. Yet cloud centric deployment of large Artificial Intelligence (AI) models often produces high task latency, lacks offline capability in communication denied environments, and requires centralizing sensitive data, raising privacy and sovereignty risks. Edge AI moves perception, prediction, and decision logic closer to the data producers via compression, collaborative inference, and split learning, thereby reducing latency, bandwidth, and exposure while enabling graceful operation during disconnections. This paper provides a panoramic view and a common understanding of edge intelligence tailored to civil aviation. We firstly articulate the operational motivations for edge AI, and then review recent techniques for edge inference and edge learning. We then introduce the organizational computing paradigms and the respective configurations in civil aviation environments; finally, we describe the emerging applications and the future research trends of edge intelligence in civil aviation. We argue that a refined edge solution can complement cloud foundations to deliver low latency, privacy preserving, and resilient AI services across the civil aviation lifecycle.

</details>


### [101] [Symbol and Footprint Database for Electronic Components by Agentic Recognition and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19767)
*Yichen Shi,Yuzhi Liu,Zhuofu Tao,Li Huang,Yuhao Gao,Ting-Jung Lin,Lei Hel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SFgen，一种基于多模态大语言模型的电子元器件符号与封装自动识别与生成流程，实现86%的符号生成准确率和80%的封装生成准确率，并构建了包含1000个元器件的SFnet数据库，为PCB设计的自动生成奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PCB设计中，工程师手动创建符号和封装并设计原理图，耗时且易出错。现有方法缺乏对元器件符号和封装的自动识别与生成能力，亟需一种高效、准确的自动化方案。

Method: 利用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）构建SFgen智能体流程，实现电子元器件符号和封装的识别与自动生成。

Result: 符号生成准确率86%，封装生成准确率80%；基于SFgen构建的SFnet数据库目前已包含1000个元器件并持续扩展。

Conclusion: SFgen有效实现了符号与封装的自动生成，SFnet数据库为PCB自动化设计提供了基础资源，展现了MLLMs在电子设计自动化领域的应用潜力。

Abstract: A rich and recognizable component library is the cornerstone of printed circuit board (PCB) design and generation. Traditionally, engineers manually create symbols and footprints and design PCB schematics, which is time-consuming and error-prone. Leveraging multimodal large language models (MLLMs), we develop SFgen, an agentic recognition and generation flow of symbol and footprint for electronic components. SFgen achieves 86% accuracy for symbol generation and 80% accuracy for footprint generation. We use the SFgen method to create SFnet, a database of symbols and footprints. It now has 1000 components and is expanding constantly, which lays the foundation for automatic generation of PCB designs.

</details>


### [102] [Silent Failures in Multimodal Agentic Search:A Diagnostic Taxonomy and Cross-Judge Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19793)
*Zhengxian Wu,Junjie Gao,Kai Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究多模态智能搜索系统中的"隐性失败"问题，提出六类故障分类法，并构建轨迹级诊断管道。实验表明表面准确率高估了真实轨迹正确性，隐性失败具有能力依赖性且会转移而非消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估主要关注最终答案准确率，忽略了搜索轨迹中的失败（如幻觉、矛盾、错误归因等），导致对系统可靠性的误判。

Method: 提出六类隐性失败分类法（模态捷径、幻象定位、错误证据正确答案、过度检索清洗、跨模态矛盾、来源幻觉），构建轨迹级诊断管道，在统一ReAct框架下同时评估答案正确性和证据基础质量。

Result: 在MMSearch-Plus轨迹上对四个前沿多模态模型实验表明：表面准确率一致高估轨迹级正确性；通过交叉判断验证、空白图像压力测试和工具消融实验证明隐性失败具有能力依赖性且会转移而非消失。

Conclusion: 需要超越最终答案准确率的评估范式，关注搜索过程中的隐性失败，以更全面衡量多模态智能搜索系统的可靠性。

Abstract: Multimodal agentic search systems increasingly rely on external tools to answer knowledge-intensive visual questions. However, existing evaluations mainly focus on final-answer accuracy and may miss failures in the search trajectory. In this work, we study such hidden reliability issues as silent failures. We introduce a six-category taxonomy covering modality shortcuts, phantom grounding, wrong-evidence-right-answer cases, over-retrieval laundering, cross-modal contradiction, and provenance hallucination. Based on this taxonomy, we build a trajectory-level diagnostic pipeline that evaluates both answer correctness and evidence-grounding quality under a unified ReAct-style scaffold. Experiments on MMSearch-Plus trajectories across four frontier multimodal models show that surface accuracy consistently overestimates true trajectory-level correctness. We further use cross-judge validation, blank-image stress tests, and tool ablations to show that silent failures are capability-dependent and often shift rather than disappear. Home-page: https://github.com/DingWu1021/silent-failures-multimodal-agentic-search

</details>


### [103] [Rewarding Better Thinking for LLM Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19824)
*Xubo Liu,Wenya Guo,Ruxue Yan,Xinying Qian,Ying Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Thinking Checklist Reward (TCR)，一种过程导向的奖励机制，通过将偏好对转化为样本特定的思维检查清单，并采用EMA残差公式分离出无法从结果奖励预测的思维盈余，从而在强化学习偏好对齐中提供更细粒度的推理轨迹指导。实验表明TCR在多个模型和基准上持续提升对齐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL偏好对齐方法中的代理奖励多为结果级（outcome-level），主要评估最终回复，对推理轨迹的指导有限，导致当多个回复获得相似最终分数时，信用分配粗糙且轨迹级偏好不明确。

Method: 1) 将偏好对转化为样本特定的思维检查清单（thinking checklist）；2) 用清单评估生成推理轨迹是否满足偏好暗示的考量；3) 引入指数移动平均（EMA）残差公式，分离出结果奖励无法预测的互补思维盈余。

Result: 在3个模型家族的5个模型上，TCR在多个对齐基准上持续提升性能，消融实验验证了EMA残差公式和样本特定清单监督的重要性。

Conclusion: 过程导向的思维检查清单奖励能有效补充结果级奖励的不足，提供更细粒度的轨迹级偏好信号，提升RL偏好对齐效果。

Abstract: LLM preference alignment aims to optimize models toward human preferences across diverse user instructions. Reinforcement learning has become a major post-training approach for this goal, but existing proxy rewards are often outcome-level, mainly evaluating the final response while providing limited guidance for the reasoning trajectory. This can make credit assignment coarse when multiple responses receive similar final scores, leaving trajectory-level preferences under-specified. To address this limitation, we propose Thinking Checklist Reward (TCR), a process-oriented reward for RL-based preference alignment. TCR converts preference pairs into sample-specific thinking checklists and uses them to evaluate whether the generated reasoning trace addresses the preference-implied considerations. To reduce overlap with outcome-level supervision, TCR further introduces an exponential moving average (EMA) residual formulation to isolate a complementary thinking surplus beyond what is predictable from the outcome reward. Experiments on five models from three model families show that TCR consistently improves alignment performance across diverse benchmarks, with ablations further validating the importance of EMA-based residual formulation and sample-specific checklist supervision.

</details>


### [104] [Know Your Agent: Reconnaissance-Driven Pentesting of AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19837)
*Or Zion Eliav,Eyal Lenga,Shir Bernstien,Yisroel Mirsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI代理也需要像传统渗透测试一样进行侦察，并形式化了代理侦察过程，开发了KYA框架来自动化黑盒侦察驱动的渗透测试，通过探测代理、构建目标画像并利用画像制造更强攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统渗透测试依赖侦察发现弱点、构建攻击，但AI代理缺乏类似的侦察机制，导致面对间接提示注入攻击时防御不足。

Method: 形式化代理侦察过程，识别知识资产（是什么、如何被利用、利用哪些弱点）；构建KYA框架实现黑盒侦察驱动渗透测试自动化：探测代理→构建目标画像→利用画像制造更强攻击。

Result: 在代理安全基准测试和真实编码代理上评估了KYA，并开源了KYA、基准测试和基线实现。

Conclusion: AI代理需要类似传统渗透测试的侦察流程，KYA框架通过自动化侦察驱动的渗透测试能有效发现和利用代理弱点。

Abstract: Traditional pentesting uses reconnaissance at each step to uncover unseen weaknesses, build stronger attacks, and advance the objective; we argue that AI agents require the same treatment. We formalize agent reconnaissance by modeling the process and identifying the knowledge assets it seeks to extract: what they are, how they are used, and which agent weaknesses they exploit to give adversaries leverage in indirect prompt injection attacks. We instantiate these insights in Know Your Agent (KYA), a framework that automates black-box, reconnaissance-driven pentesting by probing agents, building target profiles, and using those profiles to craft stronger attacks. We evaluate KYA on agent-security benchmarks and a real-world coding agent, and release KYA, its benchmarks, and baseline implementations for reproducibility.

</details>


### [105] [DocOps: A Verifiable Benchmark for Autonomous Agents in Complex Document Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19865)
*Jiazhen Jiang,Boxi Cao,Lingyong Yan,Yaojie Lu,Hongyu Lin,Shuaiqiang Wang,Dawei Yin,Xianpei Han,Le Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 DocOps，一个基于层次化任务分解的确定性可验证评估框架，用于系统评估 AI 智能体在复杂文档操作任务中的能力。实验发现，即使最先进的模型在处理高耦合、长序列任务时仍存在严重局限，并揭示了三种关键失败模式：长期状态追踪崩溃、浅层语义验证和结构性元数据的破坏性编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主智能体的快速发展，其可靠操作数字文档的能力对于实现通用 AI 助手和自动化复杂工作流至关重要，但现有评估体系缺乏对文档操作任务的系统性和可验证性评估。

Method: 提出了 DocOps 评估框架，基于层次化分类法将文档操作分解为原子维度和逐步升级的工作流复杂度，并系统评估了多种闭源和开源模型在不同智能体框架下的表现。

Result: 即使是最高级的前沿模型在处理高耦合、长范围任务时仍表现出明显局限；通过细粒度分析发现了三种关键失败模式：长期状态追踪崩溃、浅层语义验证和结构性元数据的破坏性编辑。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了智能体在维护全局文档一致性方面的能力边界，为未来设计鲁棒、非破坏性的复杂数字生态系统智能体提供了方向。

Abstract: As autonomous agents rapidly evolve, their ability to reliably manipulate ubiquitous digital documents has become critical for enabling general-purpose AI assistants and automating complex workspace workflows. In this paper, we introduce DocOps, a deterministically verifiable evaluation framework underpinned by a hierarchical taxonomy that deconstructs document operations inspired by real-world practices into atomic dimensions and escalating workflow complexities. Based on DocOps, we systematically evaluate representative closed- and open-source models across various agentic harnesses, revealing that even the most advanced frontier configurations still exhibit profound limitations when handling highly coupled, long-range tasks. Furthermore, a fine-grained analysis of existing agents' manipulation behaviors uncovers 3 key failure modes: long-term state tracking collapse, shallow semantic verification, and destructive editing of structural metadata. Ultimately, our work exposes the capability boundaries of agents in maintaining global document consistency, shedding light on the future design of robust, non-destructive agents for complex digital ecosystems.

</details>


### [106] [JANUS: Foreseeing Latent Risk for Long-Horizon Agent Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19913)
*Yuan Xiong,Linji Hao,Shizhu He,Yequan Wang,Lijun Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Janus框架，通过多智能体模拟合成轨迹，训练前瞻性守卫模型Vanguard，在工具使用前预判并阻止不安全动作，在四个基准测试中防护率提升15.9%，良性任务完成率提升5.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体安全研究侧重事后内容审核，缺乏对工具使用过程中长期风险的预判能力，需要前瞻性安全机制。

Method: 通过多智能体模拟合成多样化轨迹；训练共享策略包含两个耦合任务：预判任务预测安全相关的未来状态，裁决任务基于观测前缀和预判未来做出安全决策；使用CoAA-RL联合优化，以预判对下游安全判断的效用作为奖励信号。

Result: 在四个智能体安全基准上，Vanguard平均防护率提升15.9个百分点，良性任务完成率提升5.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 前瞻性安全框架能有效预判长期风险，在不牺牲任务完成率的前提下显著提升智能体安全性。

Abstract: Agent safety is moving from content moderation toward preventing operational failures before tool-using agents act. We propose Janus, a foresight-oriented framework for long-horizon agent safety that trains guards to anticipate delayed risks from partial trajectories. Janus synthesizes diverse agent trajectories via multi-agent simulation and learns a shared policy with two coupled tasks: an anticipation task that forecasts safety-relevant futures and an adjudication task that decides safety from both the observed prefix and anticipated future. The two tasks are jointly optimized with CoAA-RL, which rewards forecasts by their utility for downstream safety judgment. The resulting guard model, Vanguard, blocks unsafe actions before execution. Across four agent-safety benchmarks, Vanguard improves average protection by 15.9 percentage points over baseline guards while increasing benign task completion by 5.1 percentage points.

</details>


### [107] [Long-Term Sequential Decision Making under Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19914)
*Irmaan,Mirzanejad,Nadjet Bourdache,Abdel-Illah Mouaddib*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对有限时域MDP中基于根（坚决）风险目标（即秩相关函数）的规划问题，提出了一种免枚举、免采样的精确动态规划方法ERQDP，通过秩-分位数代理、离散化回报网格上的概率质量函数DP以及带证书的任意循环，可以高效返回带显式上下界差距的认证解，支持风险厌恶和风险寻求行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以处理有限时域MDP中非线性的秩相关风险目标（如根风险），因为这类目标破坏了Bellman最优性，传统场景树枚举方法计算代价过高，而采样方法缺乏严格的收敛保证。

Method: ERQDP方法包含三个关键部分：(1) 构建秩-分位数代理目标，使得可通过精确DP求解；(2) 在离散化回报网格上使用概率质量函数（PMF）进行DP，精确评估候选策略，并给出显式的舍入误差界；(3) 任意循环中不断细化代理，输出针对目标目标的显式上下界差距（证书），直到离散化预算用完。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，ERQDP能够返回带有认证解（或显式残差间隙）的结果，支持快速风险参数扫描，运行时显著优于传统方法，并且同时支持风险厌恶和风险寻求行为。

Conclusion: ERQDP有效解决了非线性的秩相关风险目标在MDP规划中的优化问题，提供了一种既有理论保证（含证书）又具有实际计算效率（免枚举免采样、支持参数扫描）的解决方案，为风险敏感的序列决策开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We study finite-horizon MDP planning under \emph{root-based} (resolute) risk objectives that apply a rank-dependent functional to the distribution of total returns. Such objectives are non-linear in the return distribution and generally break Bellman optimality, so direct optimization by scenario-tree enumeration is intractable. We propose \textbf{ERQDP}, an enumeration-free and sampling-free method that solves a rank--quantile surrogate via exact DP (Dynamic Programming), evaluates candidate policies exactly by DP over return Probability Mass Functions (PMFs) on a discretized return grid (with an explicit rounding bound), and refines the surrogate in an anytime loop that reports an explicit upper--lower gap (certificate) for the target objective up to discretization budgets. Across tested benchmarks, ERQDP returns certified solutions or explicit residual gaps, enables fast risk-parameter sweeps with substantial runtime gains, and supports both risk-averse and risk-seeking behaviors.

</details>


### [108] [MOF-Sleuth: Tool-Grounded Reward Alignment for Explainable Fine-Grained MOF CIF Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19935)
*Yu Liu,Zhiwei Yang,Diandian Guo,Kun Peng,Fangfang Yuan,Cong Cao,Chaozhuo Li,Zhiyuan Ma,Yanbing Liu,Guobin Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MOF-Sleuth，一个基于强化学习的CIF审核智能体，能够对金属有机框架（MOF）的晶体结构文件进行细粒度的化学证据驱动诊断，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MOF数据库中的CIF文件存在细微化学与结构错误，而传统检测方法仅提供固定检查或粗粒度标签，缺乏基于证据的解释；直接使用LLM审计则成本高且不可靠，因为化学证据隐含在原子位点记录中，需要几何、连接性、占据率、电荷计算等复杂推理。
